# TA98 circulation inventory — male baseline

**Scope correction:** the active task covers blood circulation only, excluding heart anatomy. Lymphatic and lymphoid entries in this historical baseline are outside the requested work and must not contribute to its progress count. Current blood-only evidence is in `reports/ta98-blood-circulation-inventory-installed-v12.json`.

Current installed source `d73c260cf6ed0752af5b3f420e97b11f4f180bea100aaf2f70f9082bf9a675de` resolves **525/1,149** male-applicable terms (45.692%); 624 lack direct references. This availability count includes ten carrier aliases and does not establish anatomical acceptance. The 90% availability target is 1,035 terms; 510 additional references remain necessary, before assessing all tissue and flow interfaces. The closure follows authoritative parents across chapters: the seven missing retinal venules `A15.2.04.044–.050` are already included in this denominator.

The bilateral cephalic and basilic forearm regions now expose four side-qualified controls for two additional terms, `A12.3.08.021–.022`. Four native outer surfaces are partitioned into eight disjoint source portions with literal original positions, normals, UVs and triangles; no new caps, movement or duplicated whole-source faces. Systems keeps each whole vein with its forearm/remainder controls, and Show components selects both portions. Region boundaries use provisional native radius landmarks; these source references do not establish finite vessel walls, lumens or repaired flow interfaces. Eight source-surface audits, actual Blender surface/section review, installed ordinary-body decoding and local HTTP bank checks passed. The skeleton, installed heart and female catalogue remain literal. Evidence is in local `male-native-venous-regions-v1/literal-forearm-pack-v1/`; rollback is `male-native-forearm-vein-regions-v1`. The azygos arch is not included because its existing source has 111 right-main-bronchus crossing pairs.

The complete applicable cephalic/basilic descendant inventory and adjacent forearm terms below are also retained in the existing tracked report `reports/mesh-audit/REVIEW.md`; the local JSON contract under `reports/` remains intentionally ignored. Authority: TA98 parent records, with direct [cephalic forearm](https://ifaa.unifr.ch/Public/EntryPage/TA98%20Tree/Entity%20TA98%20EN/12.3.08.021%20Entity%20TA98%20EN.htm) and [basilic forearm](https://ifaa.unifr.ch/Public/EntryPage/TA98%20Tree/Entity%20TA98%20EN/12.3.08.022%20Entity%20TA98%20EN.htm) entries. All represented rows are macroscopic vessel/network surface references; no row establishes a wall coat, opening solid or microscopic structure.

Each forearm region meets its unchanged source remainder on two literal 12-vertex rings in one shared attribute bank. These are source partition boundaries, not new solid end caps. Forearm/remainder face ownership is disjoint and exhaustive (2,640/4,272 cephalic; 1,968/2,352 basilic triangles per side). All other anatomical interfaces retain their prior source status. Source contract: pre-install catalogue `b8fdbe1c…`; emitted bank SHA256 `e5fc28c9fa601f5480f8912263ec60be276bd42df51bb8496fcc4db8046573ef`.

The installed bilateral lingual arterial families represent the same five terms `A12.2.05.015–.019` on each side, without double-counting terms. Each side has seven finite tissue cells including its native external-carotid parent, six shared open-lumen interfaces and five recipient annuli. Packed-wall, lumen and nearby-context checks passed; thickness and small-branch taper remain illustrative, with microscopic perfusion and other pre-existing ECA mouths unfinished. Source-bound installation, actual Blender sections, ordinary-body decoding and preservation evidence are in local `male-left-lingual-install-proposal-v1/` and `male-right-lingual-install-proposal-v2/`. The installed controls now resolve one 12-mesh Systems group, six owned cells per side in Show components, eight whole-tongue arterial references and the verified ipsilateral hyoglossus/sublingual-gland entries. Whole-organ references do not certify individual coat perfusion; existing bank geometry is unchanged.

The installed cystic artery `A12.2.12.031` communicates with the actual right-hepatic blood space through a finite shared-wall interface and reaches the gallbladder fundus wall without entering its lumen. Its parent and ten regional controls use the rebuilt shared bank. Reciprocal arterial-supply controls select the active 16-cell gallbladder assembly; other exterior regions retain whole-organ reference scope. Thickness and terminal perfusion remain provisional. A corrected full-context audit found **603 inherited artery–hepatic-biliary crossing pairs**: the earlier check used a nonexistent whole-duct identifier and therefore did not test that mesh. The installed v43 correction now has **zero actual whole-duct crossing pairs and zero occupied volume within the complete reconstructed duct envelope**. Source profile sizes, first eight root sections, shared annulus and fundus entry remain literal; the fixed cystic vein and checked vascular/inner-gallbladder context remain clear. All ten arterial regional selectors retain their source triangles and corner normal words. Actual emitted Blender surfaces and longitudinal sections, three explicitly verified source annuli and ordinary-body byte/bank replay are recorded in local `male-cystic-artery-correction-proposal-v43/`. This corrects the existing term and adds no availability count.

The cystic vein `A12.3.12.012` is now installed with a finite shared right-portal junction and a fundus-wall entry that does not breach the inner gallbladder surface. Its source sections retain calibre, its first eight portal sections remain literal, and its terminal annulus moves only 0.300 mm within the same recipient region. The emitted bank passes self-intersection, continuous-wall, communicating-blood-space and fixed-context checks; all eight affected portal regional controls were rebuilt from source-face ancestry. Reciprocal drainage selects the same active gallbladder assembly. Actual Blender whole-course and aligned junction sections, independent ordinary-body decoding, preserved anatomy and rollback evidence are in local `male-cystic-vein-install-proposal-v3/`. This is one source-specific macroscopic venous route; microscopic drainage, other venules and exact thickness remain provisional. Earlier failed courses remain recorded, not installed.

Bilateral native posterior lateral nasal arterial meshes resolve at `A12.2.05.089`; the existing right superior phrenic venous source resolves at `A12.3.07.010`. Its left counterpart is missing, and its 45.21 mm receiving-mouth gap remains uncorrected. Source faces, bank hashes, actual Blender views and ordinary-body hierarchy replay are recorded in local `male-native-vascular-identities-v1/installed-identities-final.json`. Those identity-only mappings added no geometry. The native M2 division tubes resolve at `A12.2.07.053` (pars insularis); they do not establish the distinct fine insular arteries `A12.2.07.054`, which remain missing.

Two further reviewed identities use four existing native sources: bilateral PCA parieto-occipital branches `A12.2.07.101`, and bilateral fibular calcaneal branches `A12.2.16.075`. These do not establish new flow junctions. The calcaneal mouth center is 13.08 mm from the upstream native fibular surface; its immediate lateral-malleolar parent `.074` has no distinct source, so that immediate interface remains unknown. Actual native Blender views, disambiguation tests and unchanged-bank evidence are in local `circulation-census-v1/two-term-adoption-v1/`. Historical baseline rows below are retained for comparison.

This is the complete source and control census before circulatory reconstruction. It establishes availability and missing work; it accepts no vessel geometry, wall, lumen, blood-flow continuity or organ interface.

## Bounded native aortic-root prototype contract

The local root prototype uses the complete `A12.2.03` inventory below. Its scoped parts are the ascending-aortic finite wall (`A12.2.03.001`), three sinus **spaces** (`A12.2.03.002`), a supravalvular boundary **curve** (`A12.2.03.003`), the bulb wall **region** (`A12.2.03.004`), and the existing right/left coronary mouths (`A12.2.03.101`, `.201`). Sinus spaces and attachment curves are not duplicate solids. Three interleaflet/commissural sectors belong to the same finite wall and share their chart boundaries; they do not pretend that separate source leaflet endpoints coincide. Existing distal coronary descendants remain unchanged, not newly reconstructed by this prototype.

The whole assembly is a paired inner/outer root wall, three sinus regions, the native ascending-aortic course from source station 14 distally, and the fixed native coronary courses. The transition preserves the actual distal-aortic and coronary mouth coordinates. The basal cardiac host opening and the three source-qualified cusp attachments remain explicitly pending; neither a source overlap nor a closed leaflet volume proves a joined hinge. All installed heart poses, cusp surfaces, free myocardial walls and the skeleton remain fixed. The prototype is local and uninstalled until wall/lumen, context, native Blender surfaces and uncapped sections pass. A prototype thickness is illustrative, not an established histological measurement. Construction, exact source inputs, partial geometry and any failed gates are retained under `.local-models/geometry-workbench/heart/`.

Male source SHA256: `1cdb70649f10bdb523beb38f1c0d09167e2344a02c3d8ce74d7f40739a777d39`. Full machine-readable term, binding, suppressed alternative and source-bank evidence: [reports/ta98-circulation-inventory.json](reports/ta98-circulation-inventory.json).

## Scope and denominator

The full canonical cardiovascular root `A12.0.00.000` has 1,301 descendants including itself. The canonical heart subtree `A12.1.00.001` contributes 101 excluded terms. Twelve further pericardial chapter terms have canonical mediastinal/pericardial parents and are also recorded as excluded heart-associated context. Coronary arteries and cardiac veins remain in the vascular inventory.

The following baseline table and term rows retain the earlier broad census for traceability. Lymphatic vessels, valves, capillaries, trunks, ducts and lymphoid organs/nodes are excluded from the active work. The blood-only inventory retains missing blood-circulation microscopic features, variants, containers, aliases and sex qualifications.

| Scope | All sex terms | Male applicable | Active direct references | Male without direct reference | 90% male target |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| vascular_excluding_heart | 1200 | 1171 | 492 | 679 | 1054 |
| lymphoid_companion_separate | 215 | 213 | 77 | 136 | 192 |
| complete_union | 1415 | 1384 | 569 | 815 | 1246 |
| arteries | 705 | 688 | 314 | 374 | 620 |
| veins | 435 | 423 | 172 | 251 | 381 |

The direct-reference numerator is an **availability upper bound**, not a count of correct distinct anatomy. Whole-carrier aliases are marked; parent-only placement is not exact identity. Named source candidates without a binding are listed separately rather than called absent meshes. The frontend resolver includes reviewed identities, source metadata/name mappings, top-level `ta98TermBindings`, installed typed controls and the actual TA98 hierarchy.

Representation classes are explicit construction-planning categories based on authoritative entity type and anatomical names. They are not proof that the required layer, microscopic tissue or opening has been constructed. A point requires a source-bound point, a surface requires a bounded surface, a boundary requires a curve, and an opening must not become a solid plug.

## Primary terminology references

[IFAA TA98 chapter 12](https://ifaa.unifr.ch/Public/EntryPage/PDF/TA98%20Chapter%2012.pdf) identifies the cardiovascular and lymphatic-vessel terminology. [IFAA TA98 chapter 13](https://ifaa.unifr.ch/Public/EntryPage/TA98%20Tree/TA98%20EN/13%20TA98%20EN.htm) identifies the separate lymphoid organs and regional node groups. The local authority cache and its explicit reviewed parent corrections supply the complete enumerated canonical closure.

## 2026-10-23 superficial cerebral venous source feasibility (male)

The complete corrected authority closure under `A12.3.06.002` contains37 male-applicable terms, including its cross-chapter retinal descendants. This inventory retains canonical parents; orbital and retinal branches remain separate work, rather than being invented as cortical veins. Direct frontend source bindings below describe identity availability only. The registry-only inventory omits some effective frontend identities, so the table uses the current source-bound installed census.

The proposed first family is superficial middle cerebral vein `.009`, inferior anastomotic vein (Labbe) `.010`, and superior anastomotic vein (Trolard) `.012`. Both anastomoses canonically parent to`.009`. These require finite3D tubular walls, not reference points, brain-surface patches or renamed whole cerebral meshes.

Primary variant evidence: [Tomasi et al.,2022](https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/surgery/articles/10.3389/fsurg.2021.817002/full),21 brains/42 hemispheres, identifies five patterns. A codominant pair with weak Sylvian veins is a possible illustrative TypeIV (14.3%), not a universal pattern; Sylvian-dominant TypeIII was42.9%. No source variant is established yet.

Source inspection rejects direct local-network transfer:22 open boundary components, nearest opening-center to native sinus surface30.966mm, and superior extent37.333mm above native cortex bounds. The two-component source also has four nonmanifold edges,two zero-area faces,two duplicate faces and one winding fault. Copying that network precisely is disallowed; it is retained only as unregistered orientation evidence.

Actual ZA superior-sagittal/transverse recipients each have two12-control open ends and no demonstrated separate finite inner wall or cortical side ostia. Measured native landmark-to-sinus surface gaps are precentral10.423mm and superior-parietal1.085mm toSSS; superior-temporal28.766mm and angular38.443mm toleft transverse. These are triangle-distance witnesses, not established vein paths or clinical measurements. No ostium is filled with a disk.

Construction remains pending reviewed courses and recipient blood-space/side-ostium definition. Skeleton, installed heart, current vascular banks, cortex and ordinary body bytes remain unchanged. Three bounded feasibility attempts used0.10s(failed import setup),1.52s(source inventory) and0.73s(measurement). Local evidence: `male-superficial-cerebral-veins-feasibility-v1/{authority-inventory,source-inspection,measured-feasibility,construction-contract}.json`.
